B)))) t butterflyAddSub_spec : IsUniformFinTM2 tmUniformButterflyAddSub 9 16 2 ∧ ∀ w A B : ℕ, B ≤ A → A < 2 ^ w → ∃ t ≤ 8 * w + 6, Turing.EvalsToInTime tmUniformButterflyAddSub.step ⟨some .popA, default, threeTapeWorkStk [] (encodeNatBits w A) (encodeNatBits w B)⟩ (some ⟨none, default, twoTapeStk (encodeNatBits (w + 1) (A + B)) (encodeNatBits w (A - B))⟩) t bitMulAdd_spec : IsUniformFinTM2 tmUniformBitMulAdd 7 16 2 ∧ ∀ (mBit : Bool) (w acc B : ℕ), acc < 2 ^ w → B < 2 ^ w → ∃ t ≤ 5 * w + 6, Turing.EvalsToInTime tmUniformBitMulAdd.step ⟨some .popMulBit, default, threeTapeWorkStk [mBit] (encodeNatBits w acc) (encodeNatBits w B)⟩ (some (Turing.haltList tmUniformBitMulAdd (encodeNatBits (w + 1) (acc + if mBit then B else 0)))) t condSubStep_spec : IsUniformFinTM2 tmUniformCondSubStep 8 16 2 ∧ ∀ w R D : ℕ, R < 2 ^ w → D < 2 ^ w → ∃ t ≤ 7 * w + 3, Turing.EvalsToInTime tmUniformCondSubStep.step ⟨some .popA, default, threeTapeWorkStk [] (encodeNatBits w R) (encodeNatBits w D)⟩ (some ⟨none, default, twoTapeStk (encodeNatBits w (if D ≤ R then R - D else R)) [decide (D ≤ R)]⟩) t

IntMul/FastDFT.lean [IntMul] :: private theorem normDFT1D_packRealComplex_sq_div_two_I {R : ℕ} (a b : Fin R → ℝ) (u : Fin R) : (normDFT1D (packRealComplex a b) u * normDFT1D (packRealComplex a b) u) / (2 * Complex.I) = normDFT1D (fun j => (a j : ℂ)) u * normDFT1D (fun j => (b j : ℂ)) u - (Complex.I / 2) * (normDFT1D (fun j => (a j : ℂ)) u * normDFT1D (fun j => (a j : ℂ)) u - normDFT1D (fun j => (b j : ℂ)) u * normDFT1D (fun j => (b j : ℂ)) u)

IntMul/FastDFT.lean [IntMul] :: private theorem sum_fin_linearConvCoeff_mul_expTwoPiI (n A B : ℕ) (u : Fin (rootR0 n)) : (∑ m : Fin (rootR0 n), (linearConvCoeff (chunkBits n) (numChunks n) A B m.val : ℂ) * expTwoPiI (((m.val * u.val : ℕ) : ℝ) / (rootR0 n : ℝ))) = (∑ i ∈ Finset.range (numChunks n), (natChunk (chunkBits n) A i : ℂ) * expTwoPiI (((i * u.val : ℕ) : ℝ) / (rootR0 n : ℝ))) * (∑ j ∈ Finset.range (numChunks n), (natChunk (chunkBits n) B j : ℂ) * expTwoPiI (((j * u.val : ℕ) : ℝ) / (rootR0 n : ℝ)))
IntMul/FastDFT.lean [IntMul] :: private theorem normDFT1D_linearConvCoeff_eq (n A B : ℕ) (hA : A < 2 ^ n) (hB : B < 2 ^ n) (u : Fin (rootR0 n)) : normDFT1D (fun m : Fin (rootR0 n) => (linearConvCoeff (chunkBits n) (numChunks n) A B m.val : ℂ)) u = (rootR0 n : ℂ) * (normDFT1D (fun i : Fin (rootR0 n) => (natChunk (chunkBits n) A i.val : ℂ)) u * normDFT1D (fun j : Fin (rootR0 n) => (natChunk (chunkBits n) B j.val : ℂ)) u)

IntMul/TMArithStages.lean [IntMul] :: theorem loopStepsAux_restoringDivPureStep_eq (w b : ℕ) (hb : 0 < b) : ∀ (u : List Bool) (st : {r : ℕ // r < b} × List Bool × List Bool), loopStepsAux (restoringDivPureStep b hb) (fun _ => 13 * w + 38) u st = u.length * (13 * w + 39) + 1

IntMul/TMArithStages.lean [IntMul] :: theorem reverse_encodeNatBits_succ_eq (n x : ℕ) : (encodeNatBits (n + 1) x).reverse = decide ((x / 2 ^ n) % 2 = 1) : : (encodeNatBits n (x % 2 ^ n)).reverse
IntMul/TMArithStages.lean [IntMul] :: theorem reverse_encodeNatBits_add_two_eq (n x : ℕ) : (encodeNatBits (n + 2) x).reverse = decide ((x / 2 ^ (n + 1)) % 2 = 1) : : decide (((x % 2 ^ (n + 1)) / 2 ^ n) % 2 = 1) : : (encodeNatBits n (x % 2 ^ n)).reverse
IntMul/TMArithStages.lean [IntMul] :: theorem cons_encodeNatBits_eq (n x : ℕ) (b0 : Bool) : b0 : : encodeNatBits n x = encodeNatBits (n + 1) (2 * x + b0.toNat)
IntMul/TMArithStages.lean [IntMul] :: theorem cons_cons_encodeNatBits_eq (n x : ℕ) (b0 b1 : Bool) : b0 : : b1 : : encodeNatBits n x = encodeNatBits (n + 2) (4 * x + 2 * b1.toNat + b0.toNat)
IntMul/TMArithStages.lean [IntMul] :: theorem four_pow_eq_two_pow_mul_two_pow : ∀ k : ℕ, 4 ^ k = 2 ^ k * 2 ^ k

IntMul/TMCompiler.lean [IntMul] :: def encodeDelimWord : List Bool → List Bool
IntMul/TMCompiler.lean [IntMul] :: @[simp] theorem encodeDelimWord_nil : encodeDelimWord [] = [true, false]
IntMul/TMCompiler.lean [IntMul] :: @[simp] theorem encodeDelimWord_cons (b : Bool) (xs : List Bool) : encodeDelimWord (b : : xs) = false : : b : : encodeDelimWord xs
IntMul/TMCompiler.lean [IntMul] :: @[simp] theorem length_encodeDelimWord (xs : List Bool) : (encodeDelimWord xs).length = 2 * xs.length + 2
IntMul/TMCompiler.lean [IntMul] :: def encodeWordStack : List (List Bool) → List Bool
IntMul/TMCompiler.lean [IntMul] :: @[simp] theorem encodeWordStack_nil : encodeWordStack [] = []
IntMul/TMCompiler.lean [IntMul] :: @[simp] theorem encodeWordStack_cons (w : List Bool) (ws : List (List Bool)) : encodeWordStack (w : : ws) = encodeDelimWord w ++ encodeWordStack ws
IntMul/TMCompiler.lean [IntMul] :: @[simp] theorem encodeWordStack_append (ws₁ ws₂ : List (List Bool)) : encodeWordStack (ws₁ ++ ws₂) = encodeWordStack ws₁ ++ encodeWordStack ws₂

IntMul/TMKronecker.lean [IntMul] :: theorem bmOddPermCombineStep_computesInTime {k1 q W : ℕ} (s B i : ℕ) (hi : i < k1) (hs : s ≤ B) (gs : List (Fin (2 ^ (i + 1)) → FixedComplex q)) (hgs_len : gs.length = 2 ^ (k1 - 1 - i)) (tW2 tW3 tW4 tW5 tSch : List Bool) : let M := 2 ^ i let rot := M - s % M let q_rem := s / M let fE : (Fin (2 ^ (i + 1)) → FixedComplex q) → List (FixedComplex q) := fun g => (List.finRange M).map (oddPermFn i s (eFn i g)) let fO : (Fin (2 ^ (i + 1)) → FixedComplex q) → List (FixedComplex q) := fun g => (List.finRange M).map (oddPermFn i s (oFn i g)) let blks := gs.flatMap (fun g => [fE g, fO g]) ∃ t ≤ 2 ^ k1 * (15000 * W + 40000) + 2000 * B + 2000, bmOddPermCombineStep.ComputesInTime (mkTapes8 (encodeDelimWord (encOddPermCombState W ((M, (rot, q_rem)), blks))) [] [] tW2 tW3 tW4 tW5 tSch) (mkTapes8 (encodeDelimWord (encOddPermCombState W (oddPermCombineStep ((M, (rot, q_rem)), blks)))) [] [] tW2 tW3 tW4 tW5 tSch) t
IntMul/TMKronecker.lean [IntMul] :: def bmOddPermSplitRun : BitMachine
IntMul/TMKronecker.lean [IntMul] :: theorem bmOddPermSplitRun_computesInTime {q : ℕ} (k1 s B W : ℕ) (hs : s ≤ B) (f : Fin (2 ^ k1) → FixedComplex q) (tW2 tW3 tW4 tW5 tSch : List Bool) : let col := (List.finRange (2 ^ k1)).map f let splitOut := (splitBlocks^ [k1]) [col] ∃ t ≤ (k1 + 1) * (2 ^ k1 * (2500 * W + 6000) + 100 * B + 500), bmOddPermSplitRun.ComputesInTime (mkTapes8 (encodeDelimWord (encPair encUnary (encPair encUnary (encList (encComplex W))) (k1, (s, col)))) [] [] tW2 tW3 tW4 tW5 tSch) (mkTapes8 (encodeDelimWord (encList (encList (encComplex W)) splitOut)) [] [] tW2 tW3 tW4 tW5 tSch) t
